Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on martingales structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 552. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 584 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_552(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 552. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^584(S, F) is trivial. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
